# 上云 API 2 与上云 API 能力集对比

## 1. 功能能力总览

#### 设备绑定与组织

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 3 条,上云 API 2 共 8 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">airport&#95;organization&#95;bind</code><br>设备绑定到组织</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceManageModule.unbindDevice</code><br>解绑设备</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">airport&#95;bind&#95;status</code><br>获取设备绑定信息</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceManageModule.modifyDeviceName</code><br>修改设备名称</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">airport&#95;organization&#95;get</code><br>查询设备绑定对应的组织信息</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceManageModule.getDeviceDetailInfo</code><br>获取设备详细信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceManageModule.getDeviceList</code><br>获取设备列表</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceManageModule.getDockDeviceStatus</code><br>获取机场设备状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceManageModule.getDroneDeviceStatus</code><br>获取飞机设备状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceManageModule.listenDeviceTopoChanged</code><br>监听设备拓扑变化</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceManageModule.listenWorkspaceDeviceChanged</code><br>监听工作空间设备变化</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 飞机姿态

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 7 条,上云 API 2 共 4 条</summary>

<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">data</code><br>消息内容</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenDroneFlysafeDatabaseVersion</code><br>监听飞机限飞数据库版本信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">wireless&#95;link&#95;topo</code><br>图传连接拓扑</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenAircraftHomeDistance</code><br>监听飞机归航点距离</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">self&#95;converge&#95;coordinate</code><br>自收敛坐标</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenDroneModeCodeReason</code><br>监听飞机模式码变更原因</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">offline&#95;map&#95;enable</code><br>离线地图开关</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenAircraftLimitsInfo</code><br>监听飞机限制信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">is&#95;near&#95;area&#95;limit</code><br>是否接近限飞区</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenAircraftComplianceInfo</code><br>监听飞机合规信息</td></tr>

#### 飞机电池

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 3 条,上云 API 2 共 2 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">battery</code><br>飞行器电池信息</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenAircraftBatteryInfo</code><br>监听飞机电池信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">serious&#95;low&#95;battery&#95;warning&#95;threshold</code><br>严重低电量告警</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenAircraftBatteryWarningThresholds</code><br>监听飞机电池告警阈值</td></tr>

### 2.3 控制权与实时链路

#### 控制权管理 / 接管

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 6 条,上云 API 2 共 5 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">cloud&#95;control&#95;auth</code><br>本遥控器授权云控列表</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ControlAuthModule.acquirePayloadControlAuthority</code><br>获取负载控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">flight&#95;authority&#95;grab</code><br>飞行控制权抢夺</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ControlAuthModule.acquireFlightControlAuthority</code><br>获取飞行控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">cloud&#95;control&#95;release</code><br>释放云端控制</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ControlAuthModule.listen&#42;(控制权来源/状态)</code></td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">cloud&#95;control&#95;auth&#95;request</code><br>请求授权云端控制</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ControlAuthModule.releaseFlightControlAuthority</code><br>释放飞行控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">control&#95;source</code><br>当前控制源</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ControlAuthModule.releasePayloadControlAuthority</code><br>释放负载控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">cloud&#95;control&#95;auth&#95;notify</code><br>请求授权结果通知</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### DRC实时链路建立 / 管理

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 5 条,上云 API 2 共 8 条</summary>

<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">query&#95;method&#95;data</code><br>fly_to 任务信息查询（多机场景）</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DrcModule.listenDrcDelayInfo</code><br>监听 DRC 链路延迟信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;initial&#95;state&#95;subscribe</code><br>DRC初始状态订阅</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenHsiInfo</code><br>监听HSI信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">heart&#95;beat</code><br>DRC-心跳</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DrcModule.listenLinkStatus</code><br>监听DRC链路状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">hsi&#95;info&#95;push</code><br>DRC-避障信息上报</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DrcModule.listenDrcDeviceHeartBeat</code><br>监听 DRC 设备心跳（确认链路活跃状态）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">delay&#95;info&#95;push</code><br>DRC-图传链路延时信息上报</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlyToPointModule.listenFlyToTaskEvent</code><br>监听指点飞行任务事件</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DrcModule.connectDrcLink</code><br>建立DRC链路连接</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DrcModule.drcRtcQualityTest</code><br>执行 RTC 链路质量测试（检测延迟/丢包等）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DrcModule.disconnectDrcLink</code><br>断开DRC链路连接</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### DRC模式切换

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 2 条,上云 API 2 共 2 条</summary>

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;mode&#95;exit</code><br>退出指令飞行控制模式</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DrcModule.disconnectDrcLink</code><br>断开DRC链路连接</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

<a id="m04"></a>

### 2.4 飞行控制

#### 一键起飞(A起B连)

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 2 条,上云 API 2 共 2 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">takeoff&#95;to&#95;point(services指令)</code><br>一键起飞</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">TakeoffModule.listenCommandTaskEvent</code><br>监听指令任务事件</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">takeoff&#95;to&#95;point&#95;progress(events进度)</code><br>一键起飞结果事件通知</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">TakeoffModule.takeoff</code><br>一键起飞</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">rth&#95;mode</code><br>返航高度模式设置值</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenAircraftRthInfo</code><br>监听飞机返航信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">current&#95;rth&#95;mode</code><br>返航高度模式当前值</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenAircraftCurrentRthMode</code><br>监听飞机当前返航模式</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">rth&#95;altitude</code><br>返航高度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightSettingsModule.setRthAltitude</code><br>设置返航高度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">return&#95;home</code><br>一键返航</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.returnHome</code><br>返航</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">return&#95;specific&#95;home</code><br>指定home点的返航</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.returnHomeCancel</code><br>取消返航</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">remaining&#95;power&#95;for&#95;return&#95;home</code><br>返航预留电量</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">device&#95;exit&#95;homing&#95;notify</code><br>设备返航退出状态通知</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">return&#95;home&#95;info</code><br>返航信息</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">return&#95;home&#95;cancel</code><br>取消返航</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 指点飞行

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 4 条,上云 API 2 共 4 条</summary>

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">fly&#95;to&#95;point</code><br>flyto 飞向目标点</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlyToPointModule.updateTargetPoints</code><br>更新指点飞行目标点</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">fly&#95;to&#95;point&#95;progress</code><br>flyto 执行结果事件通知</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlyToPointModule.start</code><br>启动直播（同步等待结果）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">fly&#95;to&#95;point&#95;stop</code><br>结束 flyto 飞向目标点任务</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlyToPointModule.drcStop</code><br>停止</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### POI兴趣点环绕

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 5 条,上云 API 2 共 7 条</summary>

<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">poi&#95;circle&#95;speed&#95;set</code><br>飞行控制—POI 环绕速度设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PoiModule.getTask</code><br>查询当前 POI 任务信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">poi&#95;mode&#95;enter</code><br>飞行控制—进入 POI 环绕模式</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PoiModule.listenFlightPoiProgress</code><br>监听 POI 环绕飞行实时进度（完成百分比）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">poi&#95;mode&#95;exit</code><br>飞行控制—退出 POI 环绕模式</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DockOsdModule.listenDockAlternateLandPoint</code><br>监听机场备降点</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">poi&#95;status&#95;notify</code><br>飞行控制—POI 环绕状态信息通知</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PoiModule.listenPoiTaskEvent</code><br>监听POI任务事件</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">alternate&#95;land&#95;point</code><br>备降点</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PoiModule.updateSpeed</code><br>更新环绕速度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PoiModule.enter</code><br>进入 POI 环绕模式</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PoiModule.exit</code><br>退出 POI 环绕模式</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 飞行参数设置

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 6 条,上云 API 2 共 5 条</summary>

<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">commander&#95;mode&#95;lost&#95;action</code><br>指点飞行失控动作</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenAircraftLimitsInfo</code><br>订阅飞机限制类信息状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">commander&#95;flight&#95;height</code><br>指点飞行高度</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.setHeightLimit</code><br>设置飞机限高</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">height&#95;limit</code><br>飞行器限高</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightSettingsModule.setOutOfControlAction</code><br>设置失控动作</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">is&#95;near&#95;height&#95;limit</code><br>是否接近设定的限制高度</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightSettingsModule.setFlightSmartPlanMode</code><br>设置智能规划模式</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">rc&#95;lost&#95;action</code><br>遥控器失控动作</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightSettingsModule.setFlightTaskHeight</code><br>设置飞行任务高度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">exit&#95;wayline&#95;when&#95;rc&#95;lost</code><br>【废弃】航线失控动作</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 紧急停桨 / 迫降

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 4 条,上云 API 2 共 5 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">emergency&#95;stop&#95;state</code><br>紧急停止按钮状态</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DockOsdModule.getEmergencyStopState</code><br>急停状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drone&#95;emergency&#95;stop</code><br>DRC-飞行器急停</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DockOsdModule.listenDockEmergencyStopState</code><br>监听机场急停状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;force&#95;landing</code><br>强制降落</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.drcEmergencyLanding</code><br>紧急迫降（就地降落，不自动返航）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;emergency&#95;landing</code><br>紧急降落</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.drcForceLanding</code><br>强制着陆（忽略当前状态强制落地）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.drcEmergencyStop</code><br>紧急刹停(DRC)</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 飞行终止系统FTS(降落伞/停桨)

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 3 条,上云 API 2 共 3 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">fts&#95;config</code><br>设置 FTS 服务器配置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.listenDrcParachuteInfo</code><br>监听降落伞状态信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">fts&#95;info</code><br>FTS 信息</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.drcOpenParachute</code><br>开伞指令（紧急开启降落伞）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drone&#95;propeller&#95;stop&#95;v1</code><br>触发 FTS 停桨</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.drcEmergencyStop</code><br>紧急刹停(DRC)</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 其他飞控(跟随/悬停等)

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 2 条,上云 API 2 共 5 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;follow&#95;mouse&#95;parameter&#95;set</code><br>DRC实时操控: drc_follow_mouse_parameter_set</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.listenDrcOsdInfo</code><br>监听 DRC 通道的飞机 OSD 信息（高频率实时数据）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.drcSetFollowMouseParameter</code><br>设置跟随鼠标的移动速度参数</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.drcSetDroneHover</code><br>设置悬停时长（单位秒，0=持续悬停）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightControlModule.drcCameraLookAt</code><br>通过 DRC 通道控制相机看向目标</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

<a id="m05"></a>

### 2.5 航线任务

#### 任务创建与管理

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 1 条,上云 API 2 共 7 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">flighttask&#95;create</code><br>创建航线任务（废弃）</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.getDeviceRecommendation</code><br>获取设备推荐（根据航线和策略推荐最优设备）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.getFlightTaskList</code><br>获取航线任务列表</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.getInFlightTaskTopos</code><br>查询指定无人机当前正在飞行的任务拓扑信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.listenTaskTopoChanged</code><br>监听任务拓扑变更（任务分配/状态变化时触发）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.createFlightTask</code><br>创建航线任务</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.setRoamingStationSn</code><br>设置漫游目标机场 SN</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.startRoaming</code><br>启动漫游飞行（飞机在多机场间漫游）</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 任务准备 / 取消

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 4 条,上云 API 2 共 3 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">flight&#95;setup&#95;exception&#95;notify</code><br>机场任务准备异常通知</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DroneOsdModule.listenFlightTasksException</code><br>监听飞行任务异常</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">flight&#95;setup&#95;abort</code><br>取消准备中的任务</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.createFlightTask</code><br>创建航线任务</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">flighttask&#95;prepare</code><br>下发任务</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.updateFlightTaskStatus</code><br>更新任务状态（0=待开始，6=挂起）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">flighttask&#95;undo</code><br>取消任务</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 任务执行

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 6 条,上云 API 2 共 4 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">flighttask&#95;resource&#95;get</code><br>任务资源获取</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskModule.getFlightTaskDetail</code><br>获取任务详情</td></tr>

<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FlightTaskRecordModule.exportFlightRecords</code><br>导出飞行记录</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 试飞任务

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 0 条,上云 API 2 共 2 条</summary>

<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="2" style="background:#fafbfc;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:middle;text-align:center;color:#6b7280;font-size:13px">不支持</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">TestFlightModule.generateTryFlightWayline</code><br>根据项目 ID 和参数自动生成试飞航线</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">TestFlightModule.createTestFlightTask</code><br>创建试飞任务（用于新机场/新区域首次飞行前安全验证）</td></tr>
</table>

</details>
