### 2.6 相机与云台

#### 拍照控制

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 11 条,上云 API 2 共 13 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;photo&#95;storage&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—照片存储设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcIntervalTakePhotoStatus</code><br>监听定时拍照状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;interval&#95;photo&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—设置定时拍</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcCameraPhotoInfo</code><br>监听相机拍照信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;photo&#95;format&#95;set</code><br>红外照片格式设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcPanoramaStatus</code><br>监听全景拍摄状态(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">photo&#95;storage&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—照片存储设置</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetPhotoSize</code><br>设置照片分辨率/尺寸</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;photo&#95;size&#95;set</code><br>DRC实时操控: drc_camera_photo_size_set</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetIntervalTakePhoto</code><br>配置定时拍照参数（间隔时间等）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;photo&#95;take</code><br>负载控制—开始拍照</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraPhotoFormat</code><br>设置照片格式（JPEG/RAW/JPEG+RAW）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;photo&#95;take</code><br>负载控制—开始拍照</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetPhotoStorageSettings</code><br>设置照片存储配置(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;photo&#95;take&#95;progress</code><br>上报拍照进度</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSmartPhotoTakePhoto</code><br>智能拍照（自动选择最优曝光参数）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;photo&#95;info&#95;push</code><br>拍照信息上报</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcStartPanorama</code><br>开始全景拍摄(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;photo&#95;stop</code><br>负载控制—停止拍照</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcIntervalTakePhoto</code><br>启动定时拍照</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;photo&#95;stop</code><br>负载控制—停止拍照</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcTakePhoto</code><br>拍照(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcStopPanorama</code><br>停止全景拍摄(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcStopTakePhoto</code><br>停止当前拍照操作</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 录像控制

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 11 条,上云 API 2 共 10 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">video&#95;storage&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—视频存储设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcCameraPreRecordedInfo</code><br>监听相机预录制信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;video&#95;resolution&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—视频分辨率设置</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcLiveVideoInfo</code><br>监听直播视频信息(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;video&#95;storage&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—视频存储设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetPreRecordedTime</code><br>设置预录制时长</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;assistant&#95;video&#95;switch&#95;set</code><br>DRC实时操控: drc_assistant_video_switch_set</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetVideoResolution</code><br>设置视频分辨率（如 4K/2.7K/1080P）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;assistant&#95;video&#95;mode&#95;set</code><br>DRC实时操控: drc_assistant_video_mode_set</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetVideoStorageSettings</code><br>设置视频存储配置(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;pre&#95;recorded&#95;status&#95;set</code><br>DRC实时操控: drc_pre_recorded_status_set</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetAssistantVideoMode</code><br>设置辅助影像模式</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;pre&#95;recorded&#95;time&#95;set</code><br>DRC实时操控: drc_pre_recorded_time_set</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetPreRecordedStatus</code><br>设置预录制（倒带录制）开关</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;recording&#95;start</code><br>负载控制—开始录像</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcStartRecord</code><br>开始录像(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;recording&#95;start</code><br>负载控制—开始录像</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetAssistantVideoSwitch</code><br>开启/关闭辅助影像</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;recording&#95;stop</code><br>负载控制—停止录像</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcStopRecord</code><br>停止录像(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;recording&#95;stop</code><br>负载控制—停止录像</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
</table>

</details>

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;focal&#95;length&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—变焦</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetLinkageZoom</code><br>设置联动变焦</td></tr>

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;focus&#95;value&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机对焦值设置</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraFocusMode</code><br>设置对焦模式（自动/手动）</td></tr>

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;focus&#95;mode&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机对焦模式设置</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcZoomAtTarget</code><br>变焦到目标(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;focus&#95;value&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机对焦值设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcCameraPointFocusAction</code><br>点击屏幕指定位置触发对焦</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;frame&#95;zoom</code><br>负载控制—框选变焦</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcCameraAimAtTarget</code><br>相机瞄准指定目标（云台自动转向）</td></tr>

#### 曝光参数

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 8 条,上云 API 2 共 12 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;exposure&#95;mode&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机曝光模式设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcCameraIsothermPolygon</code><br>监听等温线多边形信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;exposure&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机曝光值调节</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraExposureValue</code><br>设置曝光补偿值</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;aperture&#95;value&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机光圈</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetIsothermLowerUpperRange</code><br>设置等温线上下限温度范围</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;shutter&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机快门</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraApertureValue</code><br>设置光圈值</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;iso&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—ISO设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraShutter</code><br>设置快门速度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;mechanical&#95;shutter&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—机械快门</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraIso</code><br>设置 ISO 感光度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;exposure&#95;mode&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机曝光模式设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraMechanicalShutter</code><br>设置机械快门开关</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;exposure&#95;set</code><br>负载控制—相机曝光值调节</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraExposureMode</code><br>设置曝光模式（自动/光圈优先/快门优先/手动）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetIsothermColorRenderingType</code><br>设置等温线颜色渲染类型</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetIsothermRenderingPolygon</code><br>设置等温线多边形渲染区域</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetIsothermEnable</code><br>开启/关闭等温线功能</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetIsothermRoiBasedRenderingEnable</code><br>开启/关闭等温线 ROI 区域渲染</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetIrMixRate</code><br>设置可见光与红外的混合比例</td></tr>

<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetThermalSuperResolutionEnable</code><br>开启/关闭红外超分辨率</td></tr>

</details>

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;look&#95;at</code><br>负载控制—Look At</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcCameraMeasureTarget</code><br>监听相机测量目标(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">camera&#95;screen&#95;split</code><br>负载控制—分屏</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcCameraMediaStorage</code><br>监听相机媒体存储信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;look&#95;at</code><br>负载控制—Look At</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcCameraSettableCapability</code><br>监听相机可配置能力</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;screen&#95;drag</code><br>负载控制—画面拖动控制</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcCameraVisibleCapability</code><br>监听相机可见光能力</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;screen&#95;split</code><br>负载控制—分屏</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDrcLiveviewWorldRegion</code><br>监听实时画面世界坐标(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">osd&#95;info&#95;push</code><br>DRC-高频 osd 信息上报</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.listenDroneCameraWatermarkSettings</code><br>监听无人机相机水印设置推送</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;camera&#95;osd&#95;info&#95;push</code><br>摄像头 osd 推送</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraScreenSplit</code><br>设置分屏显示模式</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCameraWatermark</code><br>设置视频水印</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcResetCameraSettings</code><br>重置相机所有设置为默认值</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSetCountDownAction</code><br>设置倒计时自动操作（倒计时拍照等）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSwitchLiveLens</code><br>切换直播镜头(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.lookAtTarget</code><br>云台朝向目标</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">CameraControlModule.drcSendCameraScreenDrag</code><br>发送相机屏幕拖拽指令（云台跟随）</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

<a id="m07"></a>

### 2.7 负载扩展(PSDK)

#### 喊话器

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 15 条,上云 API 2 共 16 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">speaker&#95;play&#95;mode&#95;set</code><br>喊话器-设置播放模式</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkResourceModule.getPsdkSpeakerResourceList</code><br>获取PSDK喇叭资源列表</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;speaker&#95;tts&#95;set</code><br>喊话器—TTS喊话设置</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkControlModule.listenPsdkSpeakerAudioPlayStartProgress</code><br>监听喊话器音频开始播放的进度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">speaker&#95;play&#95;volume&#95;set</code><br>喊话器-设置音量</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkControlModule.listenPsdkSpeakerTtsPlayStartProgress</code><br>监听喊话器 TTS 开始播放的进度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;speaker&#95;play&#95;volume&#95;set</code><br>喊话器—设置音量</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkControlModule.listenDrcPsdkStateSpeakerInfo</code><br>监听PSDK喇叭状态(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;speaker&#95;play&#95;mode&#95;set</code><br>喊话器—模式设置</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkControlModule.listenDrcPsdkSpeakerPlayProgressInfo</code><br>监听PSDK喇叭播放进度(DRC)</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">speaker&#95;audio&#95;play&#95;start</code><br>喊话器-开始播放音频</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkResourceModule.createPsdkSpeakerResource</code><br>创建PSDK喇叭资源</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">speaker&#95;tts&#95;play&#95;start</code><br>喊话器-开始播放TTS文本</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkResourceModule.uploadSpeakerAudioFile</code><br>上传喇叭音频文件</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">speaker&#95;replay</code><br>喊话器-重新播放</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkControlModule.speakerPlayModeSet</code><br>设置喇叭播放模式</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;speaker&#95;replay</code><br>喊话器—重新播放</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkControlModule.speakerTTSSet</code><br>设置喇叭TTS配置</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drc&#95;speaker&#95;tts&#95;play&#95;start</code><br>喊话器—播放TTS文本</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkControlModule.speakerPlayVolumeSet</code><br>设置喇叭音量</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">speaker&#95;tts&#95;play&#95;start&#95;progress</code><br>喊话器-tts播放进度通知</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PsdkControlModule.speakerAudioPlayStart</code><br>喇叭音频播放开始</td></tr>

### 2.8 直播与图传

#### 推流控制

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 2 条,上云 API 2 共 2 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">live&#95;start&#95;push</code><br>命令设备开始推流到外部媒体服务器</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">LiveModule.start</code><br>启动直播（SDK客户端拉流,同步等待结果）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">live&#95;stop&#95;push</code><br>命令设备停止外部推流</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">LiveModule.stop</code><br>停止直播</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

#### 拉流 / 订阅

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 0 条,上云 API 2 共 4 条</summary>

<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="4" style="background:#fafbfc;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:middle;text-align:center;color:#6b7280;font-size:13px">不支持</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.start</code><br>开始直播拉流</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.startWithURL</code><br>获取直播拉流地址（不自动拉流）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.stopWithURL</code><br>停止设备推流（后端停流）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.subscribeLiveStream</code><br>订阅直播视频帧数据</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td rowspan="11" style="background:#fafbfc;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:middle;text-align:center;color:#6b7280;font-size:13px">不支持</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.subscribeAiSeiInfo</code><br>订阅 AI 检测 SEI 信息（目标检测框）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.subscribeArSeiInfo</code><br>订阅 AR SEI 信息（相机姿态与位置）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.subscribeAssistantSeiInfo</code><br>订阅智能辅助 SEI 信息（自动朝向等）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.subscribeEisSeiInfo</code><br>订阅 EIS（电子防抖）SEI 信息（裁剪区域坐标）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.subscribeFovSeiInfo</code><br>订阅 FOV SEI 信息（画幅尺寸与焦距，见 FovSeiInfo）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.unSubscribeAiSeiInfo</code><br>取消订阅 AI 检测 SEI 信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.unSubscribeArSeiInfo</code><br>取消订阅 AR SEI 信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.unSubscribeAssistantSeiInfo</code><br>取消订阅智能辅助 SEI 信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.unSubscribeEisSeiInfo</code><br>取消订阅 EIS SEI 信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.unSubscribeFovSeiInfo</code><br>取消订阅 FOV SEI 信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">PullStreamModule.unSubscribeLiveStream</code><br>取消订阅直播视频帧数据</td></tr>

<a id="m09"></a>

### 2.9 媒体与建模

#### 媒体上传管理

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 2 条,上云 API 2 共 3 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">file&#95;upload&#95;callback</code><br>媒体文件上传结果上报</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">MediaServiceModule.listenMediaFileDeviceUploadMessage</code><br>订阅设备媒体回传通知（设备上传文件后触发）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">direct&#95;file&#95;upload&#95;callback</code><br>媒体文件直传结果上报</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">MediaServiceModule.uploadGeoMediaFile</code><br>上传地理媒体文件（带有地理坐标信息的图片/视频）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">MediaServiceModule.mediaUploadCallback</code><br>媒体文件上传完成后通知服务端</td></tr>

### 2.10 空域与安全

#### 电子围栏 / 解禁授权

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">flight&#95;areas&#95;sync&#95;progress</code><br>自定义飞行区文件同步状态</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">SafetyServiceModule.uploadUnlockLicenseFile</code><br>上传解锁证书文件</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">unlock&#95;license&#95;switch</code><br>启用/禁用设备的单个解禁证书</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">SafetyServiceModule.switchUnlockLicense</code><br>切换解锁证书</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">SafetyServiceModule.checkUnlockLicense</code><br>检查解锁证书</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">SafetyServiceModule.clearDeviceUnlockLicense</code><br>清除设备解锁证书</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">SafetyServiceModule.submitUnlockLicense</code><br>提交解锁证书</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">SafetyServiceModule.syncCustomizedFlightAreas</code><br>同步自定义飞行区域</td></tr>
</table>

</details>

<a id="m11"></a>

### 2.11 设备运维

#### 机场远程调试(开关舱/重启等)

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 28 条,上云 API 2 共 25 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">register&#95;white&#95;list</code><br>服务命令: register_white_list</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.getManualBatterySwapProgress</code><br>查询手动换电进度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">battery&#95;store&#95;mode&#95;switch</code><br>电池运行模式切换</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.getEsimOperatorSwitchProgress</code><br>查询 eSIM 运营商切换进度</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ephemeris&#95;download</code><br>服务命令: ephemeris_download</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DockOsdModule.listenDockDroneBatteryMaintenanceInfo</code><br>监听机场无人机电池维护信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">cloud&#95;environment&#95;switch</code><br>服务命令: cloud_environment_switch</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.setBatteryStoreMode</code><br>电池存储模式设置</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">command&#95;send</code><br>服务命令: command_send</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.setLowPowerSwitch</code><br>设置低电量省电模式</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">debug&#95;mode&#95;open</code><br>调试模式开启</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DockControlModule.setDockCoverState</code><br>设置机场舱盖状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">debug&#95;mode&#95;close</code><br>调试模式关闭</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DockControlModule.setDockChargeState</code><br>设置机场充电状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">alarm&#95;state&#95;switch</code><br>机场声光报警开关</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DockControlModule.setDockSupplementLightState</code><br>设置机场补光灯状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drone&#95;open</code><br>飞行器开机进度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.openDrone</code><br>开启飞行器</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">supplement&#95;light&#95;open</code><br>打开补光灯</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.openDebugMode</code><br>打开调试模式</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">esim&#95;operator&#95;switch</code><br>eSIM 的运营商切换进度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.esimOperatorSwitch</code><br>eSIM 运营商切换</td></tr>
