<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">sim&#95;slot&#95;switch</code><br>eSIM 和 SIM 切换</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.getEsimActivateState</code><br>查询 eSIM 激活状态</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">battery&#95;maintenance&#95;switch</code><br>电池保养状态切换</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.setDonglePowerSwitch</code><br>控制 Dongle 电源开关（4G/5G 模块断电节能）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">drone&#95;format</code><br>飞行器数据格式化进度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeployModule.downloadEphemeris</code><br>下载星历数据到飞机（加速 GPS 冷启动）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">sdr&#95;workmode&#95;switch</code><br>增强图传开关</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.openCharge</code><br>开启充电</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">rtk&#95;calibration</code><br>一键标定结果</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DockControlModule.changeDockCameraPosition</code><br>切换机场相机位置</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">cover&#95;close</code><br>关闭舱盖进度</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.switchAlarmLightState</code><br>声光报警开关</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">cover&#95;open</code><br>打开舱盖进度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.deviceReboot</code><br>设备重启（危险操作，生产环境请二次确认）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">charge&#95;open</code><br>打开充电进度</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.droneSdrWorkMode(String,int,…)</code><br>切换飞行器 SDR 工作模式（即时操作）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">putter&#95;open</code><br>推杆展开进度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">RtkCalibrationModule.calibrationByManual</code><br>RTK 手动标定</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">device&#95;format</code><br>机场数据格式化进度</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">RtkCalibrationModule.calibrationByNetwork</code><br>RTK 网络标定</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">esim&#95;activate</code><br>eSIM 激活进度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">RtkCalibrationModule.subscribeCalibrationProgress</code><br>订阅 RTK 标定进度回调</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">cover&#95;force&#95;close</code><br>强制关舱盖进度</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.startManualBatterySwap</code><br>触发指定槽位的手动换电流程</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">device&#95;reboot</code><br>机场重启进度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">DeviceDebugModule.openDockCover</code><br>开启舱盖（下发 + 订阅进度推送）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">putter&#95;close</code><br>推杆闭合进度</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">charge&#95;close</code><br>关闭充电进度</td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">supplement&#95;light&#95;close</code><br>关闭补光灯</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td></tr>
</table>

<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FirmwareManageModule.listenDeviceOtaProgress</code><br>实时监听设备 OTA 升级进度（按 SN 订阅）</td></tr>

<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FirmwareManageModule.submitFirmwareInfo</code><br>提交固件信息</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">FirmwareManageModule.checkFirmwareStatus</code><br>检查固件状态</td></tr>

<a id="m13"></a>

### 2.13 地图与态势

#### 地图元素管理(标注/共享)

<details style="margin:14px 0;border:1px solid #dcdfe4;border-radius:8px;background:#fafbfc;padding:0 0 2px">
<summary style="cursor:pointer;padding:11px 15px;font-size:13.5px;color:#3d4450;font-weight:600;user-select:none"><b>协议对照</b> — 上云 API 共 3 条,上云 API 2 共 8 条</summary>

<table style="width:100%;table-layout:fixed;border-collapse:collapse;margin:14px 0;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #d1d9e0;color:#1f2328">
<tr><th style="width:50%;background:#e8eaed;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API</th><th style="width:50%;background:#cdeee2;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:9px 12px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;color:#1f2328">上云 API 2</th></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">mapElementCreate/Update/Delete (WS推送)</code></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ElementModule.listElementHierarchy</code><br>查询元素层级列表</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">mapGroupRefresh (WS推送)</code><br>刷新地图元素组推送</td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ElementModule.listenElementCreate</code><br>监听元素创建事件</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">POST/PUT/GET/DELETE /map/api/v1/.../elements (HTTP)</code></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ElementModule.listenElementUpdate</code><br>监听元素更新事件</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ElementModule.listenElementDelete</code><br>监听元素删除事件</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ElementModule.createElement</code><br>在地图上创建元素（标注点/线/面）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ElementModule.setElementVisibility</code><br>设置元素可见性（显示/隐藏）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f7f8f9;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#edf9f5;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ElementModule.updateElement</code><br>更新元素属性（坐标/颜色/名称等）</td></tr>
<tr><td style="background:#f0f1f3;border:1px solid #d4d8dd;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"></td><td style="background:#e0f4ed;border:1px solid #b4e0d0;padding:8px 12px;vertical-align:top;line-height:1.6;color:#1f2328;word-break:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere"><code style="background:#e8ecf1;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#0550ae">ElementModule.deleteElement</code><br>删除地图元素</td></tr>
</table>

</details>
